class buf1_loop_body:

    index0 = 0
    index1 = 512*z0 + z1
    index2 = z0
    def body(self, ops):
        get_index = self.get_index('index0')
        load = ops.load('seed_cuda_0', get_index)
        constant = ops.constant(65535, torch.int32)
        bitwise_xor = ops.bitwise_xor(load, constant)
        get_index_1 = self.get_index('index1')
        index_expr = ops.index_expr(get_index_1, torch.int32)
        rand = ops.rand(bitwise_xor, index_expr, torch.float32)
        constant_1 = ops.constant(0.1, torch.float32)
        gt = ops.gt(rand, constant_1)
        to_dtype = ops.to_dtype(gt, torch.float32)
        get_index_2 = self.get_index('index1')

        constant_2 = ops.constant(1.1111111111111112, torch.float32)
        mul_1 = ops.mul(mul, constant_2)
        mul_2 = ops.mul(mul_1, mul_1)
        get_index_3 = self.get_index('index2')
        reduction = ops.reduction('buf1', torch.float32, torch.float32, 'sum', get_index_3, mul_2)
        return reduction

        mul_1 = ops.mul(mul, constant_2)
        get_index_4 = self.get_index('index3')
        load_3 = ops.load('buf1', get_index_4)

        div = ops.div(load_3, constant_3)
        constant_4 = ops.constant(1e-06, torch.float32)
        add = ops.add(div, constant_4)
        sqrt = ops.sqrt(add)
        reciprocal = ops.reciprocal(sqrt)

        mul_3 = ops.mul(load, mul_2)

        get_index_2 = self.get_index('index2')
        load_2 = ops.load('buf295', get_index_2)
